- **Implementation:** Paired scoreboard and Brier slice committed 96e381ee0 and released in A; task1.2 partial. **Acceptance:** Historical local 35-case independent acceptance; later deployed receipt reconciled. **Release:** Release A 9d789c372 / dpl_J8nEZopFmW7s3XTYGK4SP9uktzQn recorded READY with 5/5 smoke; not freshly observed.

New scoped implementation and independent review before integration; model changes require frozen-cohort bias, RMSE and forecast-binned decile calibration per head, not MAE alone.
